<image-desc>A page from a traditional Chinese woodblock-printed book. The page is bounded by a heavy black outer border. The main body of the page is divided into eight empty vertical columns by thin ruled lines. On the far right edge, in the area typically used for the fold (banxin), there is printed text. The upper portion contains the title "金史" (History of Jin) followed by several partially visible characters. The middle portion, framed by "fish-tail" (yu’er) marks, identifies the volume and page as "卷五之三" (Volume 5, Part 3).</image-desc>
